# Peach Horizon - Kazu Guitar One Hour Journey

Original instrumental background-music journey with 17 evolving movements, based on the gentle mood of the provided Peach Horizon reference.
Six tracks; recorded Kazu Guitar is the lead and fingerpicked accompaniment. Gentle spacious passages alternate with syncopated, 8th-note and 16th-note passages.
Full piece is exactly 60:00 in notation at 92 BPM, 4/4: 1380 bars. Fixed tempo, deliberately variable perceived pulse and note density.
Loop design: the final bars gradually become softer and sparser, leaving a low G7-to-C turnaround rather than a long silence. Loop at the bar boundary, not after a renderer-inserted tail.

## Timeline
- 00:00 - Intro (low-density, guitar)
- 00:20 - Movement 1. Horizon Opens
- 03:49 - Movement 2. Light on the Window
- 07:18 - Movement 3. Drifting Clouds
- 10:46 - Movement 4. Garden Steps
- 14:15 - Movement 5. Paper Lanterns
- 17:44 - Movement 6. Blue Afternoon
- 21:13 - Movement 7. Train by the Coast
- 24:41 - Movement 8. Passing Fields
- 28:10 - Movement 9. Sun Through Leaves
- 31:39 - Movement 10. Moonwater
- 35:07 - Movement 11. Quiet Streets
- 38:36 - Movement 12. River Reflections
- 42:05 - Movement 13. Stars Over the Hill
- 45:33 - Movement 14. The Long Way Home
- 49:02 - Movement 15. Rain Then Sunshine
- 52:31 - Movement 16. Golden Return
- 56:00 - Movement 17. Horizon Reprise
- 59:29 - Coda: soft landing -> G7 turnaround -> return to C major
